Row 3: ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ♛ (h6=WQ)
Row 6: ⭘ ⭘ ♟ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ (c3=BP)

If `h8g8` is the ONLY legal move, white must be in check and only this move escapes it. But the rook is at e7.
Maybe the board is oriented differently?
If Row 1 is Rank 1 and Row 8 is Rank 8:
Row 8: ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘
Row 7: ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘

Row 3: ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ♛ (h3=WQ)
Row 2: ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ♜ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ (e2=BR)
Row 1: ⭘ ⭘ ♚ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ♔ (c1=BK, h1=WK)

Now check:
WK: h1
BK: c1
WQ: h3
BR: e2
BP: c6

Is White King h1 in check?
Black Rook is at e2. It doesn't check h1.
Wait, maybe the Black Rook is at h2? No, it's the 5th column (e).
Wait, look at Row 2 again: ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ♜ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘. That's position (2, 5). In UCI, that's e2.
Look at Row 1: ⭘ ⭘ ♚ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ♔. That's position (1, 3) and (1, 8). c1 and h1.

If the legal move is `h8g8`, it means the King was at h8.
So Row 1 must be Rank 8.
Row 1: Rank 8 (c8=BK, h8=WK)
Row 2: Rank 7 (e7=BR)
Row 3: Rank 6 (h6=WQ)
Row 6: Rank 3 (c3=BP)

Wait, if the only legal move is `h8g8`, maybe the White King is in check from the Rook?
If the Rook is on e7, and the King is on h8... the Rook can't check the King.
UNLESS the Rook is actually on h7?
Let's re-read the board:
Row 1: ⭘ ⭘ ♚ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ♔
Row 2: ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ♜ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘  <- Rook is the 5th symbol. That's 'e'.
Row 3: ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ♛  <- Queen is the 8th symbol. That's 'h'.

Wait, let me look at the legal moves again.
"get_legal_moves" -> "h8g8"
This is extremely restrictive. Only one legal move.
If `h8g8` is the only move, it's likely a forced move to escape check or the only move possible.
